# Documentation copy audit

Audited from staging snapshot `4d2fa26f5de0532048487a94bbe1afc744500dfe`, beginning 2026-10-13. Rebased onto `origin/staging` at `9058427155` and reviewed the 20 intervening commits before final validation. The [editorial guide](EDITORIAL_GUIDE.md) defines the final voice, terminology, organization, media, and generated-source rules.

## Original copy pass: scope and method

The inventory contains **484 entries**: 153 authored MDX pages, 28 generated CLI references, and 303 generated API operations. One authored entry is the root redirect, leaving 483 readable routes. That original pass excluded 306 integration pages from copy changes while checking their links and shared dependencies. The later complete integration review is recorded in [INTEGRATIONS_AUDIT.md](INTEGRATIONS_AUDIT.md).

Each section received a complete audit before rewriting. Reviewers read the entire assigned copy, retained a per-page ledger, compared consequential claims with the owning implementation, checked examples and limits, inspected local media, and used current primary documentation for external setup. Repeated generated schema prose was read once per unique value while retaining every operation-to-field relationship. This is a documentation accuracy review, not proof of a live hosted configuration or an exhaustive security review.

Useful facts are retained or moved to a linked canonical owner. Changes remove repeated introductions, promotional language, displaced FAQs, speculative visual placeholders, stale UI instructions, and redundant media. API and CLI prose changes occur at their canonical sources and are regenerated; identifiers, paths, permissions, schema behavior, defaults, and command options are preserved.

## Assessment and final standard

The original copy often contained useful technical detail, but placed it behind repeated benefits, broad promises, FAQs, and screenshots of ordinary controls. Pages mixed concepts, setup, reference, and troubleshooting in different orders. The final standard uses a short task-focused opening, sentence-case headings, exact visible labels, and a consistent order for each page type. It preserves detailed references where brevity would hide a requirement or failure consequence.

## Main accuracy corrections

| Area | Finding and final treatment | Implementation or canonical owner |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Workflow state and branching | Variables assign resolved values rather than calculate; Parallel shares workflow variables and unhandled errors can fail the run. Correct examples use real container names and nested result shapes. Router exposes context/reasoning rather than content; an unconnected selectedPath has an empty blockId rather than null. | `executor/handlers/variables`, `executor/execution`, workflow/block guides and shared previews |
| Runs and deployment | Draft inspection, deployed execution, sync/async receipts, token streaming, v2 framing and legacy resume differ. v2 streaming runs resume asynchronously. Workflow exports can still contain literal secrets. Thinking and tool events have separate deployment policies. Function All secrets narrows the run’s authorized environment rather than granting new access. | `lib/workflows/application`, `lib/workflows/executor`, workflow deployment guides |
| Agent and Pi | Structured output is requested, not universally guaranteed; parsed fields are not always an object in content. Cache rules vary by model. Pi includes Plan; SSH shell starts in the repository but has the account’s OS permissions. | Agent/Pi handlers and block references |
| Tables | Cells nest under row.data. Mappings populate Start fields without restricting full row context. False/empty dependencies wait; edits/output clearing can re-arm work. Completed blank results differ from incomplete runs. Expiration is scheduled deletion. | `lib/table`, workflow-column worker/UI and table guides |
| Credentials and permissions | Credential Groups grant allowed integration types. Personal secrets have grant-resolution nuances without a current sharing control. Explicit empty SCIM groups govern nobody; empty inheritance groups can broaden scope. Workspace keys differ from human principals. | Credential/permission-group use cases, platform and enterprise guides |
| Logs and retention | Refresh loads a stable snapshot; search text matches run IDs, not arbitrary run content. Current trace uses stacked sections. Run-backed alert cooldowns differ from the per-workflow No Activity cooldown. Cloud Free defaults to 30 days, paid and billing-disabled modes have no plan default, and configured cleanup is deletion rather than an archive. | Logs UI/filter implementation, cleanup dispatcher and Data retention |
| Costs | Billing mode is distinct from hosting location. Model-block cost objects and Logs API cost fields use USD while Billing/run-resource endpoints and displayed logs use credits. Unsupported purchase/voice instructions are removed; live Max limit conflicts stay qualified. | Cost contracts, billing configuration and Costs |
| Search | Supported sources retain their specific access/query/read limits. Drive’s native Sheets reads need Sheets API; OAuth setup has a canonical owner. Confluence overflow relevance pages are partial and have no cursor; native CQL supports continued paging. GitLab legacy search is unavailable; Zoom is deployment-enabled rather than universally present. | Search adapters, provider setup and Search guides |
| Chat and desktop | Recurring Chat work uses deployed workflow schedules; worker behavior is source evidence, not a live Cloud guarantee. Desktop selected-folder reads, browser uploads, Full file access and OS shell privileges are distinct. Native Mac-app control requires its deployment gate, device opt-in and app approval; Chat Find searches loaded transcript windows. | Current external-worker snapshot, desktop tools/settings and Chat/Desktop guides |
| Self-hosting | Deployment-wide schedule caps, background job inventory, Node workers, secrets/TLS/origins, storage durability and billing-disabled limits are scoped. Chart 1.11.8 is a verified published example, not a live installation claim. | Compose/Helm, config and operator guides |
| MCP | JSON operations only; read operations can have POST bodies. Catalog search is bounded without a cursor, offline refresh expires, and a result-size error can follow a committed write. | MCP catalog/dispatch/auth and MCP guides |
| Generated requests | Original rendered defaults had 17 invalid JSON bodies out of 135. Media-level validated examples and valid fork/push/pull/import examples correct these without changing the input contracts. | OpenAPI generator and owning contracts |
| CLI | Resource/lifecycle organization keeps comprehensive command lookup, flags, aliases and confirmation consequences. Inline code preserves literal pipes, backslashes and nested ticks. | CLI command metadata and documentation generator |
| Output and limits QA | Qualified Function timeout/stdout limits, Agent metadata-name collisions, Evaluator fallback/coercion, abbreviated Connections outputs, and fixed table column limits. | Current Function/Agent/Evaluator handlers and Table constants |
| Final preservation review | Restored five provider-token setup links, Stop all and manual dependency behavior, regional PII identifiers and Presidio reference, named Credential references, exact paused v2 resume lookup, and the direct migration prerequisite before upgrade commands. Workflow preview code and visible labels match their examples. | Current settings, executor, table worker, PII catalog and migration owners |
| Current source qualifications | Corrected paid retention tiers, GiB storage units, Bun/Node prerequisites, SSO/Integrations labels, drain endpoint policy, reversible encryption-key mismatch, NetworkPolicy any-source default, and Pi absolute provider TTL/account limits. | Billing cleanup, settings UI, outbound profiles, encryption, chart and sandbox adapters |
| API and CLI final comparison | Truthful full connector example uses current projected fields/scopes and incremental-sync support. CLI help distinguishes credit usage, saved changes, delete scheduling, variable batches and file references. Restoring the search keyword “spent” preserves billing discovery without the false period claim. | Connector metadata/projection, command metadata and existing search ranking tests |
| Generated output safety | The real resources input exceeds Biome’s default 1 MiB cap, which previously yielded successful empty output. The formatter now explicitly caps input/output at 16 MiB, rejects process failures and nonempty-input/empty-output results, and preserves complete parsed JSON. | `scripts/format-generated-source.ts` and actual generator boundary proof |

## Independent final comparison

A fresh parallel review compared every complete original page with its final version, including examples, prerequisites, useful FAQ answers, and local media. Exact ledger reconciliation covers all **484 entries across 22 sections**, without duplicate or missing assignments. Reviewers judged each entry an improvement in clarity, organization, accuracy, or useful coverage, with **zero remaining identified copy issues** after corrections. This is a documented comparative judgment, not a guarantee that every external setup or deployed policy has been exercised. The final column retains the per-page comparison and preservation conclusions.

The final source pass also reviewed the later Chat content-delivery gate, crawler/Markdown changes, and shared file-ownership foundation from staging. Workspace access, search completeness, sharing, storage and retention wording remain supported. Newly added Project foundations are not described as a released Project Files surface. Integration landing backlinks and per-page Markdown are preserved.

## Components and asset cleanup

### October 20 visual refinement

Current source inventory after staging sync: 490 MDX files, including 308 integration pages, and 33 article image uses. Counts in the dated follow-ups below describe the checks at that stage. The final font review removed the article and footer Season overrides: body copy and navigation retain the existing Inter family, while existing shared controls retain their established typography.

#### Final pre-ship review

- Removed the partial font-family change, verified the Browser settings bottom-edge crop, and checked that all 33 image uses match their overview/detail purpose. Removed two WebP substitutions that were larger than their originals; the media viewer retains source PNGs for zoom fidelity.
- Rebased the documentation work onto current staging without unrelated release commits. Reviewed the newly added Help Scout reference, removed its repeated capability list and closing promotion, and retained authentication, webhook-signature, connector, and field details. Its action and trigger tables use the shared disclosures.
- Current-source typography checks pass at 320, 390, 768, and 1280px across Desktop, Editor, and Help Scout, with matching article/navigation font families and no document overflow. The hydrated shared media viewer preserves its background at 125% zoom, closes with Escape or image activation, and restores focus to its trigger; desktop and phone layouts were inspected in a focused component preview.
- React cleanup found no additional effect/state/query issues. API request panels stay mounted to avoid repeating syntax highlighting when switching languages; disclosure controls have 44px touch targets. Full repository validation remains assigned to CI.

### Earlier copy pass

Shared navigation and API UI labels follow sentence case while preserving acronyms and product names. Authored tabs use a local wrapper around Fumadocs selection and keyboard behavior: mixed panels keep their inset, single code fences fill the frame, and mobile tab lists scroll with 44px targets. SSO provider anchors use native explicit triggers with `aria-controls` matching their panel IDs; keyboard navigation, selection, and bookmarked activation remain native. The shared tab frame uses the actual header/TOC row offset when scrolling a bookmarked panel into view. The compact drawer uses 30px visible chip faces within 44px mobile touch targets, aligned full-width dividers, and the shared section order. TOC/header rows reserve their actual heights at mobile/tablet breakpoints; prose wraps without changing intentional code/table scrolling. API method badges use existing platform surface and text tokens instead of custom color overrides. Integration MDX content remains unchanged. The final pass removes the unused ActionMedia component and its local styling, three unused workflow-preview definitions/exports, and 111 local media files (15,187,771 bytes). Every removed asset belonged to an originally reviewed page and had no basename consumer in current repository source, including integrations and application code. Shared components and media with other consumers remain. Remote media was removed from pages only; no remote assets were deleted.

FAQ answers were folded into their relevant sections. Callouts decreased from 234 to 117; authored Image/ActionImage and Video/ActionVideo instances decreased from 178 to 23. All 36 editorial placeholders were removed. These counts cover the 153 authored entries and exclude generated reference pages and integrations.

## Page-by-page disposition

Every row below represents a fully reviewed inventory entry. “Rewritten” includes organization/voice changes; “corrected” highlights an accuracy fix; “generated” means the owner was changed and the page regenerated; “retained” means reviewed copy already met its purpose. Shared API changes include sentence-case summaries, authentication labels, nonpaged bounds, response-unit/retention qualifications, neutral domain-error examples, valid request/response rendering, and sentence-case UI headings.

## Qualified claims and separate product issues

- A repository default or flag does not prove the deployed Cloud value. Pricing, plan caps and rollout claims remain linked/qualified where live evidence is unavailable. The Max concurrent-run value conflicts with public pricing; the copy does not choose an unverified number.
- External Chat worker evidence is the read-only staging snapshot `3aec2a03379d175264c26481d25f85647297ee32`. No live worker deployment was authenticated or changed.
- Provider console procedures and client syntax were checked against primary sources. No provider account, live OAuth grant, infrastructure installation, license entitlement or production data operation was exercised.
- Uninspected remote media is unverified, not declared stale. Redundant media can be removed when written instructions preserve its purpose.
- Project file ownership, storage and realtime foundations are present in the latest staging snapshot, but the corresponding Project Files routes/editor callbacks are absent. Future Project docs must distinguish organization retention from workspace overrides. No usable Project Files surface is claimed in this rewrite.
- Separate implementation observations include missing shipped upload-session cleanup scheduling, permissive schedule validation versus the cron parser, Redis-unavailable row coordination fallback, partial MCP discovery without a completeness flag, and SDK streaming limitations. Copy describes current supported behavior; this pass does not silently change these systems.
